DELIBERATELY NOT a threshold: picking a bar tonight encodes a guess and manufactures drain work on a category we have correctly ruled non-obligating. The study instead measures whether backlog size/age at a desk PREDICTS a later correction, a stale-figure dispatch AGAINST that desk, or a reconcile. ⛔ NULL ARM CORRECTED 2026-09-11 19:1x (WALTER self-reported, Codex-flagged; the original wording was WALTER's, adopted by PROME nearly verbatim and therefore PROME's to fix): the original said 'predicts nothing ⇒ info is PROVEN free and we stop looking'. That is an OVERCLAIM — absence of a detected relationship is not evidence of no relationship, and a small or inconclusive study cannot establish that unread information has no cost. THE DEFENSIBLE NULL ARM IS A BOUNDED DECISION TO STOP MONITORING UNDER STATED CONDITIONS — n, which desks, what window, what effect size the study could have detected — never a proof of costlessness. ⚠️ WALTER's own note on why the bad version was attractive, worth keeping: 'we stop looking' is what makes a null result feel worth funding, which is exactly the pressure that inflates it; the honest version is less satisfying and is the one that survives someone asking what n was. HISTORICAL RECORD: PENDING · RULE ALREADY LIVE — the practice is adopted and was applied by hand on 9/14 (TERRY, BOND, DAEDALUS doorbelled 15:3x). This row is the MECHANIZATION only, so a miss here degrades to the hand procedure rather than to nothing. ⛔ NOT built 9/14 by choice, reasons on the record: the late-session rule (two-correction stop tripped repeatedly that day), and prome_gate.py carrying live unrepaired residue of its own (L366 ①-⑤, and L364's finding that its four BLOCK checks are NOT a class and must be treated individually) — adding a sixth check to that file at the end of a long correction chain is the shape of edit that produced L366. ‖ 🔴 **LIVE INSTANCE OF CONDITION ⑧, OBSERVED 2026-09-14 16:0x AND IT IS WORSE THAN THE CONDITION ANTICIPATED — the two signals DISAGREED on a push that SUCCEEDED.** FALCON ran `scripts/safe-push.sh`: it **PRINTED the full receipt** `Pushed. CONFIRMED: HEAD 353fead7e is on origin/master (fresh fetch).` **AND EXITED 1.** (Benign cause, named: a concurrent push train carried the ref with seven sessions live on one box.) ⛔ **CONDITION ⑧ SAYS A CLOSEOUT MUST NOT READ PUSH STATE FROM STDOUT. THIS INSTANCE SHOWS THE RECIPROCAL IS ALSO TRUE: rc IS NOT A RELIABLE SUCCESS SIGNAL EITHER.** A runner keying on **stdout** would have called it success — correct here, by luck. A runner keying on **rc** would have called it failure — wrong here, and would have sent a desk into a non-ff recovery it did not need, ON A SHARED INDEX WITH SIX OTHER LIVE SESSIONS. ⇒ **NEITHER SIGNAL IS THE CHECK.** The only thing that established the truth is what FALCON actually did **unprompted**: fresh `git fetch`, local HEAD **==** `origin/master`, `merge-base --is-ancestor` YES, and each of its five commits confirmed present on the remote. ★ **So condition ⑧ must be written as a POSITIVE requirement, not a prohibition — *verify at the remote ref*, never merely *do not read stdout*.** A prohibition on one bad signal silently blesses the other bad signal, which is `[[finding_impeachment_must_be_scoped_to_the_claim_not_the_source]]` in the construction direction. ⚠️ PROME's own `safe-push` run the same hour returned the receipt cleanly, so **this divergence is INTERMITTENT and load-dependent — it will not reproduce on a quiet box, and a test written on a quiet box will pass.** Fixture accordingly (WQ-229: test the NEIGHBOURS — this is the *concurrent activity* category, and it is the one that fired). ‖ 🔴 **THIRD FAILURE MODE, SAME DAY, DISTINCT FROM THE 387	next-PROME-process-pass	🔑 **PROCESS FINDING, UNDATED BY DESIGN — *A CORRECTION PASS IS WHERE THIS DEFECT CLASS BITES HARDEST, BECAUSE THE FIX GETS THE ATTENTION AND THE SENTENCES AROUND IT ARE ASSUMED TO HAVE BEEN CHECKED.* HENRY's generalisation, 2026-09-14, and it arrives with a clean FOUR-INSTANCE SAMPLE FROM A SINGLE DAY.** **THE FOUR, all between HENRY, BRENT, TERRY and PROME, all on 2026-09-14:** ① **the crack** — right call, dead reasoning (BRENT's exclusion survived; its products-fell evidence did not) · ② **the identity check** — right answer, non-discriminating method (an `expireDate` match alone proves nothing without a negative control) · ③ **the hazard key** — right hazard, self-invalidating condition (date-keyed, so it goes quiet before the next occurrence) · ④ **the re-key itself** — right correction, RESIDUAL BOUNDEDNESS CLAIM left standing inside it. ⭐⭐ **THE COMMON STRUCTURE, which is the transferable half: IN ALL FOUR THE *OUTPUT* WAS CHECKABLE AND THE *SUPPORT* WAS NOT — so every review touched the half that was already fine.** A figure, a verdict, a threshold: all verifiable.
